# Evidence, open team sheets — what every legal effect lets reach the log about Stat Points

Generated by `npm run catalog` · Pokemon Showdown commit `a5df8274` · Pool: 293 species, 203 abilities, 510 moves, 166 items, 84 conditions, 25 natures · Read with: `evidence-catalog.md` §1–§2
Do not edit by hand. `npm run catalog -- --check` fails when this file and a fresh run disagree.

## 0. How to read a card
Box glyphs: `✓` USED, `✗` UNUSED, `‼` UNSOUND, `↻` REBUILD-FAILED, `·` no row.
Roles: `U` user-hidden, `T` target-hidden, `H` holder-hidden, `O` other-hidden.
